\sum_{j=1}^{p}\Big[\mathbb{P}(\mu_{j}<L_{j})+\mathbb{P}(\mu_{j}>U_{j})\Big]\leq 2\sum_{j=1}^{p}\mathbb{P}\big(S_{\tau,n}^{*}>\Phi^{-1}(1-\alpha/2p)\big)=\alpha+o(1),

which completes the proof of Theorem
4.5
.

A.16
Proof of Proposition
A.1

From the definition of
f(\cdot)
, it is obvious that
|f(Y)|\leq|Y|
and
|f(Y)-Y|\leq|Y|\mathbbm{1}(|Y|>\tau)
. First for the bound for
|\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}-\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big\{(2|Y|+2\mathbb{E}|Y|)(|Y|\mathbbm{1}(|Y|>\tau)+\mathbb{E}[|Y|\mathbbm{1}(|Y|>\tau)])\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{2}}.

Similarly, we can obtain the same bound for
|\sigma_{2}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|
. Since
|f(Y)-Y|\leq|Y|\mathbbm{1}(|Y|>\tau)
, we have

\displaystyle|\mu-\tilde{\mu}|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}f(Y)-\mathbb{E}Y|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}|f(Y)-Y|\leq\mathbb{E}[|Y|\mathbbm{1}(|Y|>\tau)]\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{3}}.

As for (
A.75
), it follows by (
A.74
) that when
\tau
satisfies (
4.11
),

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}-1\Big|

\displaystyle=\frac{|\mathbb{E}[(f(Y)-\mu)^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}]|}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\mu-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}\leq\frac{(\mu-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}-\frac{A\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A(\mu-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\leq\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}.

(A.116)

Now we proceed to show (
A.76
). First for
0<x\leq 2
, it holds that
1-\Phi(2)\leq 1-\Phi(x)\leq 1
. Then we can deduce that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{1-\Phi\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}x+c\big)}{1-\Phi(x)}-1\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\Big|\Phi\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}x+c\big)-\Phi(x)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\Big[\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}-1\big)x+c\Big].

(A.117)

Recalling that
|c|=\frac{\sqrt{n}|\mu-\tilde{\mu}|}{\sigma_{1}}\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma\tau^{3}}
, and
|\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\sigma_{2}}-1|\leq|\frac{\sigma_{1}^{2}}{\sigma_{2}^{2}}-1|\leq\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}
. Therefore, it follows that for
0<x\leq 2
,

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{1-\Phi\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}x+c\big)}{1-\Phi(x)}-1\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\Big(\frac{(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}+\frac{\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma\tau^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma\tau^{3}},

(A.118)

where the last inequality is derived by using the fact that
\frac{\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma\tau^{3}}\geq\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}
for
\tau
satisfying (
4.11
). For
x>2
, it holds that when
x\leq a_{0}\frac{\tau^{3}\sigma}{\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}
,

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{1-\Phi\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}x+c\big)}{1-\Phi(x)}-1\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\Big[\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}-1\big)x^{2}+cx\Big]\exp\Big\{A\Big[\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}-1\big)x^{2}+cx\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq A\Big[\big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}-1\big)x^{2}+cx\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Ax\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{3}\sigma}.

The above results for the two cases of
0<x\leq 2
and
2<x\leq a_{0}\frac{\tau^{3}\sigma}{\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}
yield the desired result (
A.76
).

Next we proceed to prove (
A.77
). Recall that

\displaystyle R_{x}\leq A\frac{(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{n\sigma^{4}}+r_{x},

(A.119)

where

\displaystyle r_{x}=n\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\Big\{\frac{(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{(f(Y)-\mu)^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}\Big\}\mathbbm{1}\big((1+x)\frac{|f(Y)-\tilde{\mu}|}{\sqrt{n}\sigma_{1}}>1\big)\bigg].

The goal is to upper bound the exponential part. By (
A.75
) and the range (
4.11
) for
\tau
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{(f(Y)-\mu)^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq A\frac{(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{(f(Y)-\mu)^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=A\frac{(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}+\kappa_{1}(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})+\kappa_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{A}{1+\kappa_{1}(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{-1}+\kappa_{2}(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{-2}},

(A.120)

where

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}

\displaystyle=2(\tilde{\mu}-\mu)\leq\frac{A\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{3}},

\displaystyle\kappa_{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{2}^{2}+(\tilde{\mu}-\mu)^{2}\geq A\sigma^{2}-\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\tau^{6}}\geq A\sigma^{2}

for
\tau
satisfying (
4.11
). Observing that for any
x\in\mathbbm{R}
,

1+\kappa_{1}x+\kappa_{2}x^{2}\geq 1-\frac{\kappa_{1}^{2}}{4\kappa_{2}},

hence when (
4.11
) hold with large number
c_{1}
such that
\kappa_{1}^{2}\leq\kappa_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{2}}{(f(Y)-\mu)^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}}\leq A.

(A.121)

Consequently, it follows that

\displaystyle r_{x}\leq A\frac{(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}(|f(Y)-\tilde{\mu}|^{4})}{n\sigma_{1}^{4}}\leq\frac{A(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{n\sigma^{4}},

which combining with (
A.119
) yields the desired result (
A.77
).

Finally, we deal with the proof of (
A.78
) when
\tau
satisfies (
4.11
). It follows from (
A.75
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}}\Big)^{3}-1\Big|\leq\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}.

(A.122)

Since
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}(1+\frac{c}{x})
and
|c|\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}|\mu-\tilde{\mu}|}{\sigma}\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma\tau^{3}}\leq A_{0}
for some absolute constant
A_{0}
when
\tau
satisfies (
4.11
), we have
|c|/x\leq A_{0}/2
for
x>2
and
\tau
satisfying (
4.11
). In addition,

\displaystyle\Big|\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\Big|\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{x\sigma\tau^{3}},

(A.123)

and we can obtain from (
A.75
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\mathbb{E}(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{3}}{\sigma_{1}^{3}}-\frac{\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}}{\sigma^{3}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\mathbb{E}(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})^{3}-\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}|}{\sigma_{1}^{3}}+|\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}|\Big|\frac{1}{\sigma_{1}^{3}}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{3}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\Big(\frac{\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma^{3}\tau}+\frac{|\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}|(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{5}\tau^{6}}\Big)=O\Big(\frac{1}{\tau}\Big).

(A.124)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\mathbb{E}[(f(Y)-\tilde{\mu})(f(Y)-\mu)^{2}]}{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}}{\sigma^{3}}\Big|

\displaystyle=O\Big(\frac{1}{\tau}\Big).

(A.125)

Then the desired result (
A.78
) is derived by plugging in the above bounds in (
A.122
)–(
A.125
). This completes the proof of Proposition
A.1
.

A.17
Proof of Proposition
A.2

Recall the definition that
g(Y)=(Y-\mu)\mathbbm{1}\{|Y|\leq\tau\}
. First for the bound for
|\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}\{[g(Y)-{\mu}_{0}-(Y-\mu)][g(Y)-{\mu}_{0}+(Y-\mu)]\}|

\displaystyle\leq A\tau^{-2}\mathbb{E}\big\{\big(|Y|^{3}+|\mu|Y^{2}+\mathbb{E}|Y|^{3}+|\mu|\mathbb{E}Y^{2}\big)(|Y|+|\mu|+\mathbb{E}|Y|\big)\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{2}}.

Similarly, we can obtain the same bound for
|\sigma_{2}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|
. Moreover, we have

|\mu_{0}|=|\mathbb{E}g(Y)|\leq\mathbb{E}[|Y-\mu|\mathbbm{1}\{|Y|>\tau\}]\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{3}}.

As for (
A.83
), it follows by (
A.82
) that when
\tau
satisfies (
4.11
),

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\sigma_{4}^{2}}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}-1\Big|

\displaystyle=\frac{|\mathbb{E}[(g(Y)-\mu_{0})^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[(g(Y))^{2}]|}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}-\frac{A\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\mu_{0}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\leq\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}.

(A.126)

Therefore, (
A.84
) can be proved by the same procedure of proving (
A.76
).

Next we proceed to prove (
A.85
). Recall that

\displaystyle R_{x}\leq A\frac{(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{n\sigma^{4}}+r_{x},

(A.127)

where

\displaystyle r_{x}=n\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\Big\{\frac{(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{2}}{(g(Y))^{2}+\sigma_{4}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{4}^{2}}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}\Big\}\mathbbm{1}\big((1+x)\frac{|g(Y)-{\mu}_{0}|}{\sqrt{n}\sigma_{3}}>1\big)\bigg].

The key point is to upper bound the exponential part. By (
A.83
) and the range (
4.11
) for
\tau
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{2}}{(g(Y))^{2}+\sigma_{4}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{4}^{2}}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq A\frac{(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{2}}{(g(Y))^{2}+\sigma_{4}^{2}}

\displaystyle=A\frac{(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{2}}{(g(Y)-\mu_{0})^{2}+\kappa_{1}(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})+\kappa_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{A}{1+\kappa_{1}(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{-1}+\kappa_{2}(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{-2}},

(A.128)

where

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}

\displaystyle=2{\mu}_{0}\quad\mbox{satisfying}~|\kappa_{1}|\leq\frac{A\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\tau^{3}},

\displaystyle\kappa_{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{4}^{2}+({\mu}_{0})^{2}\geq A\sigma^{2}-\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\tau^{6}}\geq A\sigma^{2}

for
\tau
satisfying (
4.11
). Observing that for any
x\in\mathbbm{R}
,

1+\kappa_{1}x+\kappa_{2}x^{2}\geq 1-\frac{\kappa_{1}^{2}}{4\kappa_{2}},

hence when (
4.11
) hold with large number
c_{1}
such that
\kappa_{1}^{2}\leq\kappa_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{2}}{(g(Y))^{2}+\sigma_{4}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{4}^{2}}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}\leq A.

(A.129)

Consequently, it follows that

\displaystyle r_{x}\leq A\frac{(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}(|g(Y)-{\mu}_{0}|^{4})}{n\sigma_{3}^{4}}\leq\frac{A(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{n\sigma^{4}},

which combining with (
A.119
) yields the desired result (
A.85
).

Finally, we deal with the proof of (
A.86
) when
\tau
satisfies (
4.11
). It follows from (
A.83
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\frac{\sigma_{4}}{\sigma_{3}}\Big)^{3}-1\Big|\leq\frac{A(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\tau^{6}}.

(A.130)

Since
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}(1+\frac{\delta}{x})
and
|\delta|\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}|\mu_{0}|}{\sigma}\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma\tau^{3}}\leq A_{0}
for some absolute constant
A_{0}
when
\tau
satisfies (
4.11
), we have
|\delta|/x\leq A_{0}/2
for
x>2
and
\tau
satisfying (
4.11
). In addition,

\displaystyle\Big|\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\Big|\leq\frac{A\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{x\sigma\tau^{3}},

(A.131)

and we can obtain from (
A.83
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\mathbb{E}(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{3}}{\sigma_{3}^{3}}-\frac{\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}}{\sigma^{3}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\frac{|\mathbb{E}(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})^{3}-\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}|}{\sigma_{3}^{3}}+A[\mathbb{E}|Y-\mu|^{3}]\Big|\frac{1}{\sigma_{3}^{3}}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{3}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\Big(\frac{\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4}}{\sigma^{3}\tau}+\frac{|\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}|(\mathbb{E}|Y|^{4})^{2}}{\sigma^{5}\tau^{6}}\Big)=O\Big(\frac{1}{\tau}\Big).

(A.132)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\mathbb{E}[(g(Y)-{\mu}_{0})(g(Y))^{2}]}{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}^{2}}-\frac{\mathbb{E}(Y-\mu)^{3}}{\sigma^{3}}\Big|

\displaystyle=O\Big(\frac{1}{\tau}\Big).

(A.133)

Then the desired result (
A.86
) is derived by plugging in the above bounds in (
A.130
)–(
A.133
). This completes the proof of Proposition
A.2
.

A.18
Proof of Corollary
2.1

We apply Theorem
2.1
with
Y_{i}^{2}=\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{2}
,
c_{0}=0
and
c=0
. In this case,

\displaystyle\Psi_{x}^{*}=\exp\Big\{\frac{x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{1}^{3}}{6\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}}\Big\}\quad,L_{3,n}\leq\frac{2\mathbb{E}|X_{1}|^{3}}{\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}},\quad\delta_{x,1}\leq\frac{4(1+x)^{4}\mathbb{E}X_{1}^{4}}{n^{2}\sigma^{4}}.

For
x\leq t_{0}\sigma\sqrt{n}/4
, we have
2x/(\sigma\sqrt{n})\leq t_{0}/2
and thus

\displaystyle r_{x,1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\Big\{\min\Big(\frac{X_{1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}},\frac{2xX_{1}}{\sigma\sqrt{n}}\Big)\Big\}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{1}|>\sigma\sqrt{n})\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\Big\{\frac{2xX_{1}}{\sigma\sqrt{n}}\Big\}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{1}|>\sigma\sqrt{n})\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[e^{t_{0}X_{1}/2}\mathbbm{1}\{(1+x)|X_{1}|>\sigma\sqrt{n}\}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(1+x)^{4}n^{-2}\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{1}|^{4}e^{t_{0}X_{1}/2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq Ct_{0}^{-4}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{3t_{0}X_{1}/4}\big](1+x)^{4}n^{-2}.

Therefore it follows from Theorem
2.1
that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{P}(S_{n}>x\sigma\sqrt{n})}{1-\Phi(x)}=\exp\Big\{\frac{x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{1}^{3}}{6\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}}+O\Big(\frac{(1+x)^{4}}{n}\Big)\Big\}\Big[1+O\Big(\frac{1+x}{\sqrt{n}}\Big)\Big]

uniformly for
0<x\leq O(n^{1/2})
. Hence (
2.8
) holds for
0<x\leq O(n^{1/4})
. This completes the proof of Corollary
2.1
.

B
Additional lemmas and proofs

B.1
Proof of Lemma
A.1

Recall the notation
W_{i}=2x{X_{i}}-x^{2}{Y_{i}}^{2}
. Define

\nu_{i}=\min\left\{\frac{{X_{i}}^{2}}{{Y_{i}}^{2}+c_{0}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2}}+c_{0}x^{2}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2},2xX_{i}\right\}.

Observe that
W_{i}\leq\nu_{i}
.
For
1/4\leq\lambda\leq 3/4
and
k\in\{0,1,2,3\}
, by the elementary inequalities
|s|^{k}e^{-s}\leq c_{1}(k)
and
s^{k}e^{\lambda s}\leq c_{2}(k)e^{s}
for
s\geq 0
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{|W_{i}|^{k}e^{\lambda W_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\geq 1)\}

(B.1)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq A\mathbb{E}\{e^{(0\lor W_{i})}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\geq 1)\}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq A\big(\mathbb{E}\{e^{\nu_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\geq 1)\}+\delta_{x,i}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq AR_{x,i},

where the last inequality comes from the fact that
c_{0}x^{2}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2}\leq 1/4
for
x
satisfying (
2.7
). Additionally, there is a constant
A_{1}(k,s_{0})
such that
|s|^{k}e^{s}\leq A_{1}(k,s_{0})
if
s\leq s_{0}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{|W_{i}|^{k}e^{\lambda W_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\leq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\geq 1)\}

\displaystyle\leq A\mathbb{P}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\leq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\geq 1)

\displaystyle\leq A\delta_{x,i}.

For simplicity, denote
\mathcal{U}_{i}=\{|(1+x)X_{i}|\leq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\leq 1\}
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{k}e^{\lambda W_{i}}\}=\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{k}e^{\lambda W_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+O(1)R_{x,i},

(B.2)

where and hereafter
O(1)
is a bounded quantity.

By Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{e^{\lambda W_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}

\displaystyle=1+\lambda\mathbb{E}\{W_{i}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+\frac{{\lambda}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}

(B.3)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{\lambda}^{3}}{6}\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{3}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+O(1){\lambda}^{4}\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{4}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}.

The analysis for the terms on the right hand side of the above equality is similar. We calculate the third term for example. Notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}

\displaystyle=4x^{2}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{2}-4x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}+x^{4}\mathbb{E}\{{Y_{i}}^{4}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}

\displaystyle\quad-4x^{2}\mathbb{E}\{{X_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\geq 1)\}

\displaystyle\quad-4x^{2}\mathbb{E}\{{X_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\leq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\geq 1)\}

\displaystyle\quad+4x^{3}\mathbb{E}\{X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\leq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\geq 1)\}

\displaystyle\quad+4x^{3}\mathbb{E}\{X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\geq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\leq 1)\}

\displaystyle\quad+4x^{3}\mathbb{E}\{X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\geq 1,|(1+x)Y_{i}|\geq 1)\}.

By the basic inequality
ab^{2}\leq a^{3}+b^{3}
for
a>0,b>0
and Chebyshev’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}=4x^{2}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{2}-4x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}+O(1)\delta_{x,i}.

In the same manner,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{W_{i}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}=-x^{2}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2}+O(1)\delta_{x,i},\quad\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{3}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}=8x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}+O(1)\delta_{x,i},

\displaystyle\quad{\rm and}\hskip 28.45274pt|\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{4}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}|\leq O(1)\delta_{x,i}.

Therefore (
B.1
)–(
B.3
) yield

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda W_{i}}

\displaystyle=1-\lambda x^{2}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2}+2{\lambda}^{2}x^{2}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{2}

(B.4)

\displaystyle\qquad-2{\lambda}^{2}x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}{Y_{i}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3}{\lambda}^{3}x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}+O(1)R_{x,i}.

Because
x
satisfies (
2.7
),

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda W_{i}}-1|

\displaystyle\leq 1/2.

Furthermore, by Lemma
A.2
we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda W_{i}}-1|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq A\big[x^{4}\mathbb{E}\{{Y_{i}}^{4}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)Y_{i}|\leq 1)\}+x^{4}\mathbb{E}\{{X_{i}}^{4}\mathbbm{1}(|(1+x)X_{i}|\leq 1)\}

\displaystyle\qquad+x^{6}\mathbb{E}\{({X_{i}}^{6}+{Y_{i}}^{6})\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+R_{x,i}\big]

\displaystyle\leq AR_{x,i},

Because
|\log(1+a)-a|\leq a^{2}
whenever
|a|\leq 1/2
, it follows from (
B.4
) that

\displaystyle\log\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda W_{i}}=-\lambda x^{2}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2}+2{\lambda}^{2}x^{2}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{2}-2{\lambda}^{2}x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}{Y_{i}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3}{\lambda}^{3}x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}+O(1)R_{x,i},

which completes the proof of (
A.8
).

By the same token,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}W_{i}e^{\lambda W_{i}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\{W_{i}(1+\lambda W_{i}+\frac{{\lambda}^{2}}{2}{W_{i}}^{2})\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+O(1)R_{x,i}

\displaystyle=-x^{2}\mathbb{E}{Y_{i}}^{2}+4\lambda x^{2}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{2}-4\lambda x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}+4{\lambda}^{2}x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}+O(1)R_{x,i},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{W_{i}}^{2}e^{\lambda W_{i}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\{{W_{i}}^{2}(1+\lambda W_{i})\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+O(1)R_{x,i}

\displaystyle=4x^{2}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{2}-4x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}+8\lambda x^{3}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}+O(1)R_{x,i},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|W_{i}|^{3}e^{\lambda W_{i}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\{|W_{i}|^{3}\mathbbm{1}(\mathcal{U}_{i})\}+O(1)R_{x,i}

\displaystyle=O(1)x^{3}(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|Y_{i}|^{3})+O(1)R_{x,i}.

By a similar procedure to the proof of (
A.8
), we arrive at (
A.9
)–(
A.11
). The proof of Lemma
A.1
is completed.

B.2
Proof of Lemma
A.3

Recall the definition
m\left(\lambda\right)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\log{\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda W_{i}}}
. Note that
m(\lambda_{\delta})
is well-defined under condition (
2.3
) and
m^{\prime\prime}(\lambda)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}Var\widetilde{W}_{i}>0
for
\lambda>0
,
x\neq 0
and nondegenerate
X_{i}
and
Y_{i}
. It follows from Lemmas
A.1
and
A.2
that for
1/4\leq\lambda\leq 3/4
and
x
satisfying (
2.6
)–(
2.7
),

\displaystyle m^{\prime}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}W_{i}e^{\lambda W_{i}}/\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda W_{i}}

(B.5)

\displaystyle=(4\lambda-1)x^{2}+4{\lambda}^{2}x^{3}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}-4\lambda x^{3}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}+O(1)R_{x},

where
|O(1)|\leq A
for some absolute constant
A
.
Therefore, under (
2.6
) with sufficiently small constant
c_{1}
, we have for
|\delta(x)|\leq x^{2}/2
that

\displaystyle m^{\prime}(1/4)<x^{2}+\delta(x)<m^{\prime}(3/4),

which combined with the fact
m^{\prime\prime}(\lambda)>0
implies that the equation
m^{\prime}(\lambda)=x^{2}+\delta(x)
has a unique solution
\lambda_{\delta}
such that
1/4<\lambda_{\delta}<3/4
. Furthermore, by virtue of (
B.5
), it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\delta}=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\delta(x)}{4x^{2}}-{\lambda_{\delta}}^{2}x\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}{X_{i}}^{3}+\lambda_{\delta}x\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}X_{i}{Y_{i}}^{2}+O(1)x^{-2}R_{x},

and hence (
A.15
) follows. Again by (
B.5
) and
1/4<\lambda_{\delta}<3/4
,
1/4<\lambda_{\delta_{0}}<3/4
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\lambda_{\delta}-\lambda_{\delta_{0}}|\leq A\big(x^{-2}|\delta(x)-\delta_{0}(x)|+xL_{3,n}+x^{-2}R_{x}\big).

Therefore

\displaystyle\Big|\lambda_{\delta}-\lambda_{\delta_{0}}-\frac{\delta(x)-\delta_{0}(x)}{4x^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq A\big(|\lambda_{\delta}-\lambda_{\delta_{0}}|xL_{3,n}+x^{-2}R_{x}\big)

\displaystyle\leq A\big(x^{-2}R_{x}+|\delta(x)-\delta_{0}(x)|x^{-1}L_{2,n}\big).

Thus we complete the proof of (
A.16
).
The result (
A.17
) directly follows from (
A.8
), and (
A.18
) follows from (
A.16
) and (
A.17
). This completes the proof of Lemma
A.3
.

B.3
Proof of Lemma
A.4

In the sequel,
C,C_{1},C_{2},\ldots
are positive constants that may depend on
\omega
and
r_{0}
and may take different values at each appearance. Note that (
A.31
) is a special case of (
A.33
). Denote
Z_{i}=Y_{i}^{2}-\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}
and
G_{i}=2rxX_{i}-wrx^{2}Y_{i}^{2}
. Regarding (
A.33
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(V_{n}^{2}-1\right)^{2}e^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}G_{i}}\right\}

(B.6)

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}\Big)^{2}e^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}G_{i}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt=\mathbb{E}e^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}G_{i}}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\mathbb{E}Z_{i}^{2}e^{G_{i}}}{\mathbb{E}e^{G_{i}}}+\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{\mathbb{E}Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}}{\mathbb{E}e^{G_{i}}}\frac{\mathbb{E}Z_{j}e^{G_{j}}}{\mathbb{E}e^{G_{j}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt\leq\mathbb{E}e^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}G_{i}}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\mathbb{E}Z_{i}^{2}e^{G_{i}}}{\mathbb{E}e^{G_{i}}}+\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\mathbb{E}Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}}{\mathbb{E}e^{G_{i}}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg].

We first treat
\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}]
. Recall that
0<r<r_{0}
for some number
r_{0}
. For
x
satisfying (
2.7
),
x^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}\leq 1/16
and
c_{0}x^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}\leq 1/4
. It follows from the basic inequality
|e^{s}-1|\leq|s|e^{s\lor 0}
that

\displaystyle x^{2}\big|\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1)]\big|

(B.7)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=x^{2}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[Z_{i}\big(1+O(x|X_{i}|+x^{2}Y_{i}^{2})\big)\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1)\big]\big|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\big\{x^{2}\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}^{2}+\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2})\mathbbm{1}\{x|Y_{i}|>1\}]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+x^{2}\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}^{2}+\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2})\mathbbm{1}\{x|X_{i}|>1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1\}]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+x^{2}\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}^{2}+\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2})(x|X_{i}|+x^{2}Y_{i}^{2})\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1)]\big\}

\displaystyle\leq Cx^{3}(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|Y_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}[|X_{i}Y_{i}^{2}|])+Cx^{4}(\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq Cx^{3}(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|Y_{i}|^{3}),

where the last inequality results from Lemma
A.2
and the basic inequality
ab^{2}\leq a^{3}+b^{3}
for
a,b>0
. In addition, as
G_{i}\leq 2r<2
when
x|X_{i}|\leq 1
, we obtain for
x>3

\displaystyle x^{2}|\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|>1)]|

(B.8)

\displaystyle\leq Cx^{2}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(x|Y_{i}|>1)]+Cx^{2}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}^{2}]\mathbb{P}(x|Y_{i}|>1)

\displaystyle\leq Cx^{3}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{3}\mathbbm{1}(x|Y_{i}|>1)]\leq C\delta_{x,i}.

Moreover, as
x^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}\leq 1/16
,

\displaystyle x^{2}|\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)|

(B.9)

\displaystyle\leq x^{2}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}^{2}e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]+\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)].

As for the second error term, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1,X_{i}\leq 0)]+\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1,X_{i}>0)]

\displaystyle\leq x^{3}\mathbb{E}[|X_{i}^{3}|\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]+\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(xX_{i}>1)].

Observe that when
xX_{i}>1
,

\displaystyle e^{G_{i}}=e^{2rxX_{i}-\omega rx^{2}Y_{i}^{2}}\leq e^{2rxX_{i}}\leq e^{2xX_{i}}

and by Cauchy inequality for
0<r<r_{0}<\omega
,

\displaystyle 2rxX_{i}-\omega rx^{2}Y_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\omega r)^{1/2}xX_{i}-\omega rx^{2}Y_{i}^{2}

(B.10)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{x^{2}X_{i}^{2}}{x^{2}Y_{i}^{2}+c_{0}x^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}}+\omega rc_{0}x^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]

(B.11)

\displaystyle\leq x^{3}\mathbb{E}[|X_{i}|^{3}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]+C_{1}\mathbb{E}\Big[e^{\min\big\{\frac{X_{i}^{2}}{Y_{i}^{2}+c_{0}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}},2xX_{i}\big\}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}R_{x,i}.

As for the first error term in (
B.9
), it follows from the basic inequality
|x|^{k}e^{-\delta x}\leq C(k,\delta)
for some constant
C(k,\delta)
depending on
k
and
\delta
that

\displaystyle x^{2}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}^{2}e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\mathbb{E}[e^{2rxX_{i}-(\omega/2+r_{0}/2)rx^{2}Y_{i}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}x^{3}\mathbb{E}[x|X_{i}|^{3}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C_{1}\mathbb{E}[e^{2rxX_{i}-(\omega/2+r_{0}/2)rx^{2}Y_{i}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}(xX_{i}>1)].

As
0<r<r_{0}<\omega/2+r_{0}/2
, by a similar procedure to (
B.10
) we obtain

\displaystyle x^{2}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}^{2}e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]\leq C_{1}R_{x,i}.

(B.12)

Consequently, it follows from the bounds (
B.7
)–(
B.9
) and (
B.11
)–(
B.12
) that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}e^{G_{i}}]|\leq C\big[x(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+|Y_{i}|^{3})+x^{-2}R_{x,i}\big].

(B.13)

By similar arguments, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}^{2}e^{G_{i}}]\leq Cx^{-4}R_{x,i}.

(B.14)

In addition,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}]

\displaystyle=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\Big(\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]+\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1)]

(B.15)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|>1)]\Big).

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|>1)]\leq e^{2r_{0}}x^{3}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{3}\mathbbm{1}(x|Y_{i}|\geq 1)]\leq C\delta_{x,i},

and (
B.11
) has shown

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)]\leq CR_{x,i},

moreover, by inequality
|e^{s}-(1+s+s^{2}/2+s^{3}/6)|\leq s^{4}e^{s\lor 0}
and
G_{i}\leq 2r_{0}
when
x|X_{i}|\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1)]

(B.16)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[\big(1+G_{i}+G_{i}^{2}/2+G_{i}^{3}/6+O(G_{i}^{4})\big)\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|\leq 1,x|Y_{i}|\leq 1)\big]

\displaystyle=1+(2r^{2}-\omega r^{2})x^{2}\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{2}-2\omega r^{2}x^{3}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}Y_{i}^{2}]+\frac{4}{3}r^{3}x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{3}+O(\delta_{x,i}).

Under condition (
2.7
), we have

\delta_{x,i}\leq R_{x,i}\leq x^{3}(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|Y_{i}|^{3})+r_{x,i}\leq c_{1}+1/64.

As a result for small constant
c_{1}
,

\displaystyle 1/2\leq\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}]\leq 3/2.

Thus it follows by substituting (
B.13
) and (
B.14
) into (
B.6
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(V_{n}^{2}-1\right)^{2}e^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}G_{i}}\right\}

(B.17)

\displaystyle\leq 2\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[e^{G_{i}}]\Big(x^{-4}R_{x}+(xL_{3,n}+x^{-2}R_{x})^{3}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq Ax^{-2}R_{x}\exp\Big\{(2r^{2}-\omega r^{2})x^{2}\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{2}-2\omega r^{2}x^{3}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}Y_{i}^{2}]+\frac{4}{3}r^{3}x^{3}\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{3}+A\delta_{x,i}\Big\},

hence the desired result (
A.33
) is derived. (
A.31
) is a special case of (
A.33
), with
r=\lambda_{1}
and
\omega=1
.

Next we prove (
A.32
). We have by conditional Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Big\{\Big|W_{i}\Big(Z_{i}^{2}+2Z_{i}\sum_{i\neq j}Z_{j}\Big)\Big|e^{\lambda_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}W_{i}}\Big\}

(B.18)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|W_{i}Z_{i}^{2}e^{\lambda_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}W_{i}}\right|+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|Z_{i}W_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}Z_{j}\Big|e^{\lambda_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}W_{j}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Q_{1}+Q_{2},

where

\displaystyle Q_{1}

\displaystyle=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left|W_{i}Z_{i}^{2}e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}\right|}{\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}},

\displaystyle Q_{2}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left(|Z_{i}W_{i}|e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}\right)\Big\{\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Z_{j}\Big)^{2}e^{\lambda_{1}\sum_{j\neq i}W_{j}}\Big]\Big\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\mathbb{E}e^{\lambda_{1}\sum_{j\neq i}W_{j}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Recalling (
A.8
) and (
A.17
), we have

\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}]\leq\exp\{m(\lambda_{1})\}e^{AR_{x}}

(B.19)

and when
x
satisfies (
2.7
) with small constant
c_{1}
,

\displaystyle 1/2\leq\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}]\leq 3/2.

(B.20)

Further, through an analogous proof to (
B.13
), we obtain

\displaystyle x^{2}\mathbb{E}[|W_{i}Z_{i}|e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}|]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\big[(x^{3}|X_{i}Y_{i}^{2}|+x^{4}Y_{i}^{4}+x^{3}|X_{i}|\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}+x^{4}Y_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2})e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}\big]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\big[(x^{3}|X_{i}Y_{i}^{2}|+x^{4}Y_{i}^{4}+x|X_{i}|+x^{2}Y_{i}^{2})e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(xX_{i}>1)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C\mathbb{E}\big[(x^{3}|X_{i}Y_{i}^{2}|+x^{3}|Y_{i}|^{3}+x^{3}|X_{i}|\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}+x^{4}Y_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2})e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}\mathbbm{1}(xX_{i}\leq 1)\big]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\big[e^{\frac{10\lambda_{1}}{9}xX_{i}-\frac{9\lambda_{1}}{10}x^{2}Y_{i}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}(xX_{i}>1)\big]+Cx^{3}(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|Y_{i}|^{3}),

where in the last inequality we used the fact that
ab^{2}\leq a^{3}+b^{3}
for
a>0,b>0
and
x^{4}(\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}^{2}])^{2}\leq 2\delta_{x,i}
by Lemma
A.2
.
By (
B.10
). we obtain for
\lambda_{1}\leq 3/4
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[e^{\frac{10\lambda_{1}}{9}xX_{i}-\frac{9\lambda_{1}}{10}x^{2}Y_{i}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}(x|X_{i}|>1)\big]\leq C\mathbb{E}\Big[e^{\min\big\{\frac{X_{i}^{2}}{Y_{i}^{2}+c_{0}\mathbb{E}Y_{i}^{2}},2xX_{i}\big\}}\mathbbm{1}(xX_{i}>1)\Big].

Hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|W_{i}Z_{i}|e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}]\leq x^{-2}R_{x,i}+x(\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|Y_{i}|^{3}).

(B.21)

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(|W_{i}Z_{i}^{2}|e^{\lambda_{1}W_{i}}\big)\leq Cx^{-4}R_{x,i},

(B.22)

which together with (
B.19
) and (
B.20
) gives

\displaystyle Q_{1}\leq C_{1}\exp\{m(\lambda_{1})\}x^{-4}R_{x}e^{C_{2}R_{x}}.

As for
Q_{2}
, noting the bound (
B.19
)–(
B.21
) and (
A.31
), we have

\displaystyle Q_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(x^{-2}R_{x}+xL_{3,n})\big(\exp\{m(\lambda_{1})\}x^{-2}R_{x}e^{AR_{x}}\big)^{1/2}\big(\exp\{m(\lambda_{1})\}e^{AR_{x}}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C(x^{-2}R_{x}+xL_{3,n})\exp\{m(\lambda_{1})\}x^{{}^{-1}}R_{x}^{1/2}e^{AR_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq C\exp{m(\lambda_{1})}x^{-2}R_{x}e^{AR_{x}}.

Here we apply the fact
x^{4}L_{3,n}\leq 2\delta_{x}\leq 2R_{x}
in Lemma
A.2
to derive the last inequality. Thus (
A.32
) is proved. The proof of Lemma
A.4
is completed.

B.4
Proof of Lemma
A.5

Assume
A=1
without loss of generality, as the proof for general
A>0
is similar. By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, it is obvious that
S_{n}^{2}\leq nV_{n}^{2}
and hence
\mathbb{E}[e^{\frac{S_{n}^{2}}{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}]<\infty
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[e^{\frac{S_{n}^{2}}{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}\mathbbm{1}(b|S_{n}|>1)\Big]

(B.23)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\int_{0}^{n}e^{t}\,\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{|S_{n}|}{\sqrt{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}>\sqrt{t},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)dt

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\int_{0}^{n}e^{t}\,\Big[\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{S_{n}}{\sqrt{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}>\sqrt{t},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{-S_{n}}{\sqrt{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}>\sqrt{t},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)\Big]dt.

The integral in
0<t<1
is bounded by
eb^{p}\mathbb{E}|S_{n}|^{p}
and hence is dominated by the right hand side of (
A.57
) by Rothenthal’s inequality.
Thus it remains to consider the integral for
t>1
. Let
\nu=\frac{cB_{n}}{\sqrt{t}}
, where
c
is a large positive number. Denote
\hat{X}_{i}=X_{i}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|\leq\nu)
, then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{S_{n}}{\sqrt{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}>\sqrt{t},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)

(B.24)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{\sqrt{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}>\frac{\sqrt{t}}{2},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}{\sqrt{V_{n}^{2}+c_{0}B_{n}^{2}}}>\frac{\sqrt{t}}{2},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Big(\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{\sqrt{c_{0}}B_{n}}>\frac{\sqrt{t}}{2},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)+\mathbb{P}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)>\frac{t}{4},b|S_{n}|>1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq b^{p}e^{-\frac{c_{0}^{1/4}t}{2}}\mathbb{E}\Big[|S_{n}|^{p}\,\exp\big\{c_{0}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{t}\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{B_{n}}\big\}\Big]+b^{p}e^{-2t}\mathbb{E}\Big[|S_{n}|^{p}e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}\Big]

\displaystyle:=b^{p}e^{-\frac{c_{0}^{1/4}t}{2}}E_{1}+b^{p}e^{-2t}E_{2},

where the second inequality is obtained by Cauchy inequality. Now we turn to estimating
E_{1}
and
E_{2}
. Let
A_{n,p}=\frac{1}{2(4e^{8})^{1/p}}\max\{B_{n},(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p})^{1/p}\}
. Denote
\bar{X_{i}}=X_{i}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|\leq A_{n,p})
,
\bar{S}_{n}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{X}_{i}
and
S_{n}^{(i)}=S_{n}-X_{i}
. Observing that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}(|S_{n}|>x)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}(|\bar{S}_{n}|>x)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|S_{n}|>x,|X_{i}|>A_{n,p})

(B.25)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}(|\bar{S}_{n}|>x)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\frac{1}{2}x)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|S_{n}^{(i)}|>\frac{1}{2}x,|X_{i}|>A_{n,p}),

we have

\displaystyle E_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}[e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}\mathbbm{1}(|S_{n}|>x)]dx

(B.26)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}[e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}\mathbbm{1}(|\bar{S}_{n}|>x)]dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}[e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\frac{1}{2}x)]dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}[e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}\mathbbm{1}(S_{n}^{(i)}>\frac{1}{2}x,|X_{i}|>A_{n,p})]dx

\displaystyle\leq Q_{1}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{8}\mathbb{E}(e^{8\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{j}|>\nu)})\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{P}(|X_{i}|>\frac{1}{2}x)dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{8}({A_{n,p}})^{-p}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}[e^{8\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{j}|>\nu)}\mathbbm{1}(S_{n}^{(i)}>\frac{1}{2}x)]dx

\displaystyle\leq Q_{1}+e^{8}2^{p}\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+e^{8}2^{p}A_{n,p}^{-p}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p})E_{2}

\displaystyle\leq Q_{1}+e^{8}2^{p}\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+\frac{1}{4}E_{2},

where

Q_{1}=\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}[e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}\mathbbm{1}(|\bar{S}_{n}|>x)]dx.

The second to last inequality is based on the fact that
|x+u|^{p}
is a convex function with respect to
x
and that there exists some large constant
c
such that for
\nu=\frac{cB_{n}}{\sqrt{t}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}e^{8\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)}

\displaystyle\leq\textstyle\prod_{i=1}^{n}[1+e^{8}\mathbb{P}(|X_{i}|>\nu)]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\{\textstyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{8}\mathbb{P}(|X_{i}|>\nu)\}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\{\frac{e^{8}}{\nu^{2}}B_{n}^{2}\}\leq\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}.

As for
Q_{1}
, it holds that

Q_{1}\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}e^{-\frac{x}{A}_{n,p}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}e^{8\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)+\frac{1}{A_{n,p}}\bar{X}_{i}}dx.

(B.27)

Furthermore, as
|\bar{X}_{i}|/A_{n,p}\leq 1
, we obtain from Taylor expansion that

\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}e^{8\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)+\frac{1}{A_{n,p}}\bar{X}_{i}}

(B.28)

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left(1+e^{8}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)\right)\left(1+\frac{\bar{X}_{i}}{A_{n,p}}+\frac{e^{2}\bar{X}_{i}^{2}}{2A_{n,p}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\exp\Big\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(|X_{i}|>\nu)+\frac{1}{A_{n,p}}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\frac{1}{A_{n,p}^{2}}\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq C\exp\Big\{\frac{B_{n}^{2}}{\nu^{2}}+\frac{B_{n}^{2}}{A_{n,p}\nu}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+\frac{1}{2A_{n,p}^{2}}B_{n}^{2}\Big\}\leq A\exp\big\{\frac{t}{4}\big\},

which yields

\displaystyle Q_{1}

\displaystyle\leq A\,(A_{n,p})^{p}\,\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}e^{-x}dx

(B.29)

\displaystyle\leq A_{1}\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+(\mathbb{E}S_{n}^{2})^{\frac{p}{2}}\Big).

Consequently, it follows from (
B.26
) and (
B.29
) that

E_{2}\leq A_{2}\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+(\mathbb{E}S_{n}^{2})^{\frac{p}{2}}\Big].

(B.30)

Next we deal with
E_{1}
. Recalling that
|\hat{X}_{i}|\leq\nu=CB_{n}/\nu
, we have for large constant
c_{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\exp\Big\{c_{0}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{t}\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{B_{n}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\frac{\sqrt{t}}{c_{0}^{1/4}B_{n}}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|\mathbbm{1}(|X_{i}|>\nu)

(B.31)

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\exp\{\frac{C}{c_{0}^{1/4}}\}\frac{t}{2c_{0}^{1/2}B_{n}^{2}}\mathbb{E}X_{i}^{2}\Big]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\bigg\{\frac{\sqrt{t}B_{n}^{2}}{c_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}B_{n}\nu}+\exp\Big\{\frac{C}{c_{0}^{1/4}}\Big\}\frac{t}{2c_{0}^{1/2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\big\{\frac{t}{4}\big\}.

Denote
\hat{S}_{n}^{(i)}=\hat{S}_{n}-\hat{X}_{i}
and

Q_{2}=\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}e^{-x/a}\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big\{c_{0}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{t}\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{B_{n}}+\frac{\bar{S}_{n}}{A_{n,p}}\Big\}\Big]dx.

By a similar procedure to (
B.26
)–(
B.30
), we have for large
c_{0}

\displaystyle E_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}px^{p-1}\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big\{c_{0}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{t}\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{B_{n}}\Big\}\mathbbm{1}(|S_{n}|>x)]dx

\displaystyle\leq Q_{2}+C_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big(c_{0}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{t}\frac{\hat{S}_{n}}{B_{n}}\Big)\Big]\cdot\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{p}e^{c/c_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}}{A_{n,p}^{p}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[|S_{n}^{(i)}|^{p}\exp\Big(c_{0}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{t}\frac{\hat{S}_{n}^{(i)}}{B_{n}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq Q_{2}+C_{2}\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+\frac{1}{4}E_{1}.

Moreover, it follows from Taylor expansion that

\displaystyle Q_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(A_{n,p})^{p}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(1+\frac{\sqrt{t}}{c_{0}^{1/4}B_{n}}\hat{X}_{i}+\frac{te^{c/c_{0}^{1/4}}}{2c_{0}^{1/2}B_{n}^{2}}\hat{X}_{i}^{2}\bigg)\bigg(1+\frac{e\bar{X}_{i}}{A_{n,p}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{t/4}(A_{n,p})^{p}\leq Ce^{t/4}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+(\mathbb{E}S_{n}^{2})^{p/2}\Big].

Consequently,

E_{1}\leq C\exp\{\frac{t}{4}\}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}|^{p}+(\mathbb{E}S_{n}^{2})^{p/2}\Big],

which together with (
B.30
), (
B.24
) and (
B.23
) gives the result (
A.57
). The proof is completed.
